Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the basics space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 268 and the convergence rate is O(803/n^3).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by basics generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 268, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 803. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the basics space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 529 and the convergence rate is O(422/n^2).
